================ BAI THUC HANH BO LOC DIA CHI MAC (filter_test.c) ================
Board: MACA0 = 00:80:E1:00:00:00. Moi dong: dat bo loc -> gui 1 frame qua MAC loopback -> xem co ve RX.
hashDA      = ma hash phan mem tinh cho DA (RM tr.3121), bit 5 chon HT1R/HT0R.
HF/MADRM    = RDES2 do PHAN CUNG ghi: HF=1 -> MADRM la ma hash phan cung tinh (phai = hashDA);
              HF=0 -> MADRM la so thanh ghi MACAx da khop. DAF/SAF = 1: truot loc DA/SA.

ID  DA gui             MACPFR     HT1R     HT0R     hashDA | du kien thuc te | HF MADRM DAF SAF | ket qua

================ BAI THUC HANH BO LOC DIA CHI MAC (filter_test.c) ================
Board: MACA0 = 00:80:E1:00:00:00. Moi dong: dat bo loc -> gui 1 frame qua MAC loopback -> xem co ve RX.
hashDA      = ma hash phan mem tinh cho DA (RM tr.3121), bit 5 chon HT1R/HT0R.
HF/MADRM    = RDES2 do PHAN CUNG ghi: HF=1 -> MADRM la ma hash phan cung tinh (phai = hashDA);
              HF=0 -> MADRM la so thanh ghi MACAx da khop. DAF/SAF = 1: truot loc DA/SA.

ID  DA gui             MACPFR     HT1R     HT0R     hashDA | du kien thuc te | HF MADRM DAF SAF | ket qua

================ BAI THUC HANH BO LOC DIA CHI MAC (filter_test.c) ================
Board: MACA0 = 00:80:E1:00:00:00. Moi dong: dat bo loc -> gui 1 frame qua MAC loopback -> xem co ve RX.
hashDA      = ma hash phan mem tinh cho DA (RM tr.3121), bit 5 chon HT1R/HT0R.
HF/MADRM    = RDES2 do PHAN CUNG ghi: HF=1 -> MADRM la ma hash phan cung tinh (phai = hashDA);
              HF=0 -> MADRM la so thanh ghi MACAx da khop. DAF/SAF = 1: truot loc DA/SA.
